# Symbols the DS reference defines that the PC program is allowed not to.
#
# Format:  PATTERN  # reason
#
# PATTERN is matched with fnmatch (`*` and `?`), against the symbol name in
# the DS reference ELF. The first matching row wins. Every row needs a reason;
# a row without one is an error (tools/symbol_crosscheck.py).
#
# This is the replaced/stubbed table, not a place to hide a source file that
# was left out of the build. The entries below were generated from the real
# heartgold.us reference ELF after the host32 link: every symbol the PC
# program does not define is listed by name, with the reason it is absent.
# There are deliberately no broad `_f*`/`_ll_*`/`__*` rows: the MSL helpers the
# port now implements (hgss/compat/src/msl_runtime.c: _fadd, _fdiv, _ffix,
# _fflt, _f_lltof, _f_ulltof, _ll_mul, _ll_udiv, _s32_div_f, strnicmp,
# __lower_mapC) are present in the PC ELF and therefore match before this
# table is consulted. If one of them disappears, the cross-check fails.
#
# The same applies to every game source symbol: a new omission appears as a
# missing symbol and fails the run; only the names already recorded as
# replaced, unreferenced-and-garbage-collected, or made file-local are listed.


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- msl
